+      "raw_url": "https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/raw/5f664a16284cd0943fc8439f3d52b1db644fdd2d/docs%2Fsuperpowers%2Freports%2F2026-11-03-finite-native-custody-probe.md",

+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,11 @@\n+# Observación acotada de custodia nativa\n+\n+Base 03998d58435d7b300502e7cf417661d3be9889a0. Código d6ba42e693bb507d996b0a127052084590180f4b, rama feat/finite-native-cleanup-20261101. Fuente y pruebas sintéticas, sin despliegue ni lectura real de secretos.\n+\n+La función no montada readFiniteNativeCustodyProbe recibe el contexto del actor y lease interno. Comprueba que la identidad del actor coincida con el slot fijo y conserva los holders, reloj monotónico y plazo. Lee /tokens/verify con el lector y publicador cargados por separado, compara con los IDs fijados, exige status active, not_before cumplido y expires_on futuro, y devuelve solo status y fechas. Reutiliza readFiniteInitialOldGuard para un GET fijo del guard histórico. Los GET no llevan destino ni credenciales aportados por quien invoca. No ejecuta POST ni transacciones, no acepta un C1 ni cambia la fuente.\n+\n+La observación positiva devuelve guardVerified=true, scopeVerified=false, authorizesEffects=false y operationalReadiness=false. Un verify positivo comprueba identidad y vigencia en ese instante. El guard acredita una lectura concreta con el lector, no capacidad de todos los endpoints ni permisos de publicación. Una respuesta inválida, 401, 403, holder distinto, fecha o reloj inválido produce unavailable sin volcar datos del proveedor o las claves. El límite global es 15 segundos, con 5 segundos por verificación individual y el lease original. Los GET de token se dejan continuar y su ciclo de cuerpo/cancelación se retiene si el llamador deja de esperar; el resultado retorna dentro del plazo. Una lectura sin resolver puede continuar fuera de la observación. La lectura del guard reutiliza el comportamiento de cancelación de su implementación previa y no prueba liquidación física remota.\n+\n+RED c5fea6d6e683cc75c33189543ceb6d052b455134, Actions 41662713954: 94 PASS y 1 FAIL causal por observación ausente, usando stub cerrado. GREEN Actions 41663425525 sobre d6ba42e693bb507d996b0a127052084590180f4b: `node --test test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-workflows-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-history-schema-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-old-guard-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-gates-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-scope.test.mjs`, 95 PASS, 0 FAIL, 0 SKIP. Grupo de consumidores 83 PASS, compilación/residencia 23 PASS, lint acotado aprobado, cero artefactos.\n+\n+El actor sintético cubre identidades erróneas, estado inactivo, vencimiento, not_before futuro, guard 401, lease abortado/vencido, salida sin token y snapshot sin cambios. El token y proveedor del fixture son sintéticos. No existe ruta ni Workflow montado para ejecutar esta función con credenciales reales. Antes de usarla hay que revisar la fuente y su contexto, preparar un canal autenticado y limitado con vencimiento y cierre, y comprobar la custodia y las capacidades reales sin consumir la entrada ni iniciar una versión inactiva. La fuente de prueba finite mantiene el techo 2026-11-03T02:59:00Z vencido, que no se cambió. La extensión de ventanas o reemplazo de credenciales requiere un plan independiente. La comprobación no resuelve la calificación histórica de escritores, no activa el emisor y no acredita PC-off."

stats(){return{transactions:this.transactions,syncs:this.syncs,callbacks:this.callbacks,uploadTransactions:this.uploadTransactions,uploadStage:this.uploadStage,held:typeof this.releaseHeld==='function',bodyCancelled:this.localBodyCancelled===true};}\\n async restoreMethods(){this.releaseHeld?.();this.restore?.();}\\n\\n }export default{async fetch(request,env){const refs=await request.json();\\n  const event={payload:{operation:'finite_initial_preparation',commandId:refs.commandId,sourceRevision:refs.sourceRevision,qaVersionId:refs.qaVersionId},timestamp:new Date(refs.createdAt),instanceId:refs.initialInstanceId,workflowName:refs.workflowName};\\n  // Explicit SYN event/step delivery; actual production Workflow class/run and DO RPC.\\n  let config;const step={do:async(name,options,fn)=>{config={name,options};return fn();}};\\n  const result=await PrivateQaBootstrap.prototype.run.call({env:env},event,step);return Response.json({result,config});}};\\n\";\n  let product;try{product=await readFile('lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material.mjs','utf8');}catch{product='export function createPrivateQaFiniteInitialMaterial(){return{collect:async()=>({status:\"admin_pending\"}),assertCurrent(){throw Error(\"missing original factual producer\");}};}';}\n  const approvalDecl=\"CEILING=Date.parse('2026-11-03T02:59:00Z')\";\n  if(product.includes(approvalDecl)){assert.equal(product.split(approvalDecl).length,2);product=product.replace(approvalDecl,'CEILING='+approval);}"
+    },
+    {
+      "sha": "ec45c70d82b774740a3a5a23060ffd27342b41f6",
+      "filename": "test/mcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs",
+      "status": "added",
+      "additions": 22,
+      "deletions": 0,
+      "changes": 22,
+      "blob_url": "https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/blob/5f664a16284cd0943fc8439f3d52b1db644fdd2d/test%2Fmcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs",
+      "raw_url": "https://github.com/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/raw/5f664a16284cd0943fc8439f3d52b1db644fdd2d/test%2Fmcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs",
+      "contents_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web/contents/test%2Fmcp-qa-finite-initial-custody-probe-native.test.mjs?ref=5f664a16284cd0943fc8439f3d52b1db644fdd2d",
+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@\n+import test from 'node:test';\n+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\n+import {initialEntryHarness} from './fixtures/finite-initial-entry-world.mjs';\n+async function using(mode,fn){const f=await initialEntryHarness(mode);try{await fn(f);}finally{await f.stub.restoreMethods();await f.mf.dispose();await f.cleanup();}}\n+test('native actor observes fixed custody identity and old guard without effects',()=>using('normal',async f=>{\n+ const before=await f.stub.snapshot(),result=await f.stub.probeNativeCustody();\n+ assert.equal(result.status,'observed');assert.equal(result.guardVerified,true);assert.equal(result.authorizesEffects,false);assert.equal(result.operationalReadiness,false);\n+ assert.equal(result.reader.status,'active');assert.equal(result.publisher.status,'active');\n+ assert.deepEqual(await f.stub.snapshot(),before);assert.equal(f.uploads.length,0);assert.equal(f.restores.length,0);\n+ assert.equal(f.requests.filter(x=>x.endsWith('/tokens/verify')).length,2);\n+ assert.equal(f.requests.filter(x=>x.includes('/instances/afw-mcp-closure-d56b5e850d24cc23058a024d73bae4c830a91c1fb9659f2e96282854ccae2c04')).length,1);\n+ assert.equal(JSON.stringify(result).includes('synthetic-native'),false);assert.equal(JSON.stringify(result).includes('Bearer'),false);\n+}));\n+for(const mode of ['reader-wrong-id','publisher-wrong-id','reader-inactive','publisher-inactive','reader-expired','publisher-expired','reader-not-before','publisher-not-before'])test('native custody observation rejects '+mode,()=>using(mode,async f=>{\n+ const before=await f.stub.snapshot(),r=await f.stub.probeNativeCustody();assert.notEqual(r.status,'observed');assert.deepEqual(await f.stub.snapshot(),before);assert.equal(f.uploads.length,0);assert.equal(f.restores.length,0);\n+}));\n+test('old guard 401 stays unavailable without exposing credential or changing actor state',()=>using('normal',async f=>{\n+ f.oldGuardMode='http-401';const before=await f.stub.snapshot(),r=await f.stub.probeNativeCustody();assert.notEqual(r.status,'observed');assert.equal(JSON.stringify(r).includes('synthetic-native'),false);assert.deepEqual(await f.stub.snapshot(),before);\n+}));\n+for(const mode of ['aborted','expired'])test('unavailable parent prevents native custody reads '+mode,()=>using('normal',async f=>{\n+ const n=f.requests.length,r=await f.stub.probeNativeCustody(mode);assert.notEqual(r.status,'observed');assert.equal(f.requests.length,n);assert.equal(f.uploads.length,0);\n+}));"
+    }
+  ]
+}

+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,9 @@\n+# Ventana del titular de la preparación finita\n+\n+BASE b44fb3ccff94a87d0955bbf25dd1c549d329615e. Código y prueba e966527af2d0ecd053000ddcb6c2e538ea0a1b09.\n+\n+La configuración de la versión inicial cerrada fija AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT al tiempo inicial I0 más 900 segundos. Antes se fijaba al plazo técnico de 300 segundos, insuficiente para el presupuesto posterior de commandBy más 510 segundos. El cambio conserva I0 como origen, C0 en I0+900 s y C1 en I0+1080 s. La duración del permiso delegado, su revocación y los límites de identidad y custodia conservan sus validaciones. La configuración permanece cerrada y la carga sigue siendo inactiva.\n+\n+Prueba nativa con la implementación anterior: commit c60377f9cb4db27e3839cba28209fec052a4d632, run 41634008245, job 125910462293, 82 PASS y una única falla esperada por la diferencia entre I0+300 e I0+900. El mismo flujo con la corrección, run 41634555467, registró 83 PASS consumidores, 45 PASS focalizadas y 23 PASS compilación/residencia, cero fallos, cancelaciones u omisiones. Lint acotado pasó y no hubo artefactos. La prueba usa workerd y datos sintéticos. Persisten advertencias previas del toolchain y mensajes de disposers en fixtures.\n+\n+Esta unidad no produce activación, cápsula ni acceso del titular. Antes de emitir una activación aún hay que componer las comprobaciones de writers, inventario, custodia y publicación primaria en la misma invocación finita, guardar SEND/ACK y comprobar la publicación. El lector histórico y el puente QA producen evidencia factual, no autorización de efectos. El techo de fuente y credenciales venció el 3 de noviembre a las 02:59 UTC; antes de cualquier ensayo real se debe reconciliar una vigencia nueva y verificar el lector nativo."
+    },

+      "patch": "@@ -16,7 +16,7 @@ export function buildFiniteInitialUpload(material,baseline,bytes){\n  if(!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)||bytes.length<1||bytes.length>2097152||sha(bytes)!==m.originalContext.artifact.workerHash)fail();\n  const cfg=structuredClone(finitePublicProfileTemplate('canaryLive'));\n  cfg.bindings=cfg.bindings.map(b=>b.name.endsWith('_ENABLED')?{name:b.name,type:'plain_text',text:'false'}:b.privateSlot?{name:b.name,type:'plain_text',text:b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_CLIENT_ID'?p.clientId:b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_PROJECT_ID'?p.project.projectId:''}:b);\n- cfg.bindings.push({name:'ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.teamDomain},{name:'ACCESS_AUD',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.audience},{name:'AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT',type:'plain_text',text:new Date(m.intent.initialDeadline).toISOString()},structuredClone(p.limiter),{name:'AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_POLICY',type:'plain_text',text:''});\n+ cfg.bindings.push({name:'ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.teamDomain},{name:'ACCESS_AUD',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.audience},{name:'AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT',type:'plain_text',text:new Date(m.intent.I0+900000).toISOString()},structuredClone(p.limiter),{name:'AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_POLICY',type:'plain_text',text:''});\n  qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile({phase:'closed_ui',metadata:cfg});\n  // Every physical/secret binding must already exist under the same actual ID;\n  // the two explicitly reviewed profile references are the only exceptions."
+    },

+      "patch": "@@ -32,7 +32,7 @@ test('B2a private backup reconstructs exact baseline with bounded deterministic\n  assert.equal(uploadRows(rows).every(([,raw])=>Buffer.byteLength(raw)<=65536),true);\n  const material=JSON.parse(rows.find(([k])=>k.startsWith('afw.finite-initial-preparation.v1:material:'))[1]);\n  assert.equal(manifest.retainedIds.preconnectionId,material.preconnectionId);assert.equal(manifest.retainedIds.activationKey,material.originalContext.publication.activationKey);\n- assert.equal(f.uploads[0].data.bindings.find(b=>b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT').text,new Date(material.intent.initialDeadline).toISOString());\n+ assert.equal(f.uploads[0].data.bindings.find(b=>b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT').text,new Date(material.intent.I0+900000).toISOString());\n  assert.equal(manifest.historicalSourceRevision,f.x.receipt.sourceRevision);assert.notEqual(manifest.historicalSourceRevision,material.intent.sourceRevision);\n  for(const [key,value]of before)assert.equal(digestRows(rows.filter(([k])=>k===key)),digestRows([[key,value]]));\n  const send=JSON.parse(uploadRows(rows).find(([k])=>k.endsWith(':send'))[1]);assert.equal(send.bodyHash,sha(f.uploads[0].body));assert.ok(send.deadline-send.issuedAt<=5000);"

No provider calls, credentials, deployment, OAuth, Entry command, local clone, local build or dependencies were performed by this unit.\n+- Next: confirm exact head and causal RED; implement the full spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-03-finite-initial-capsule.md` within the original B1/A stage, preserving retained full HTTP lifecycle through cancellation. Add meaningful codec, mismatch, loss-ACK, retention, restart and no-resend tests; run focused groups, consumers and compiler/residency if the graph changes; capture exact evidence and hashes. Temporary qualifier is excluded from integration. The expired ceiling and original deadlines remain unchanged.\n+- Factual owner source, mount, cleanup handoff, live gates and PC-off integrated trial remain pending. `operationalReadiness=false`; `completePackage=false`."

+      "path": "docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-readonly-host-mount.md",
+      "status": "added",
+      "additions": 19,
+      "deletions": 0,
+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,19 @@\n+# Closed pre-Entry custody host — Unit A source report\n+\n+Status: **source-only, ready for independent review**. This is not a deployment, acceptance of the exact historical baseline, or operational readiness. `operationalReadiness=false` and `completePackage=false`. The separate controller, command, claim, provider lifecycle and promotion are Unit B and remain unimplemented.\n+\n+Base `68ae439241a2e9cfe5b7df3a841467a80820adb1`; qualified source and test revision `9a33bd5e48c47241844ea1f46764666c4448af66`. The later report-only commit does not change that code. Rollback before promotion is to revert this source unit.\n+\n+The fixed wrapper subclasses the imported baseline `IndependentClosure` and `PrivateQaBootstrap`, re-exports the baseline public handler and other classes, and adds one fixed actor RPC and one fixed Workflow operation. The actor invokes the existing `readFinitePreEntryCustody` with its own `this.ctx` and `this.env`. Correlation ID and source revision are checked against the compiled context; they are not credentials or caller attestation. Closed flags and policies, actor name, fixed Workflow instance, bounded event age, context and a strictly shaped RPC receipt are checked. The existing helper retains outstanding work and excludes concurrent owner-stage work. Neither its result nor this wrapper consumes Entry or grants admission, effects, readiness or retry authority.\n+\n+The namespace binding and reviewed closed caller paths remain the capability boundary. No claim is made that all account holders were inventoried, or that an unreviewed reachable forwarding path cannot exist. The synthetic test enumerated 27 inherited methods and called them without arguments, with no storage change or outbound request; this narrow exercise is not proof of every valid inherited call or of the actual historical module. Public fetch remained 404 in the fixture.\n+\n+Source wrapper SHA-256 `bc8d2e9ca942489631b8b9d0ee2920290a86a69b951a7506d36af66d40ea6b28`, 5889 bytes. Two credential-free builds of the fixed wrapper yielded identical 40831-byte output, SHA-256 `24c4fb81917f74617d1af9a3c05f383882bb04b104516237abe300d38fb503f5`. The baseline and context are external imports; this test bundle is not a complete deployable artifact and was not published.\n+\n+Credential-free Actions `41693118183`, job `126116536623`, on the qualified source revision completed successfully: native host/bundle group 14 passed, regression group 105 passed, both zero failures and zero skips; scoped lint passed. The exact-module hook rejected both a missing module and a same-size wrong-hash module. Native tests exercised fixed Workflow-to-actor observation, flags, expiry, holder identities, actor/correlation/source and instance selectors, malformed receipts, retained pending reads and synthetic inherited unscoped methods.\n+\n+TDD evidence: `41691331174`/job `126110383357` failed on absent wrapper source before implementation; `41691878764`/job `126112265250` demonstrated that a malformed actor receipt was initially accepted, before strict receipt validation. The earlier comparator prototype and test were removed from the final unit because they were unintegrated and insufficient; an earlier lint failure in that removed test is not a result of this qualified revision.\n+\n+The actual historical baseline `closed-rollback.mjs` is 395915 bytes, SHA-256 `4b557f04777be25f3df6dc271990d57095ace231bb7b24e2a7a10da23ada2c2f`, version `c570b60b-95dc-48e1-89f3-9c4651691dbc`. Its bytes were not available through a credential-free retained artifact here. Before any future upload, run the native test with `AFW_REQUIRE_EXACT_BASELINE=1` and `AFW_QA_INSPECTION_BASE_MODULE` pointing to the exact verified module, review inherited methods and reachable binding holders, and preserve the resulting evidence. The synthetic fixture is not equivalent.\n+\n+Unit B must separately implement and verify its distinct command/claim, pinned build and context, primary closed/provider and budget gates, one bounded Workflow observation and independent restoration/readback. Active-version configuration and script-level settings must be captured and preserved against their own baselines; equality between them is not assumed. Unexplained drift, unsafe settings mutation or inability to preserve/recover both blocks a future operation. Existing dated canary active/settings differences are not authorization to normalize either. Actor readers/publisher remain expired; no renewal, provider request, credential use, workflow dispatch or deployment occurred here."
+    },

+      "path": "docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-03-finite-readonly-preentry-custody.md",
+      "status": "added",
+      "additions": 9,
+      "deletions": 0,
+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,9 @@\n+# Custodia read-only anterior a Entry\n+\n+Base ce76c9ac3cfd5332ae8957dfd9eab8bebdccbb45. Fuente calificada a0351f403cbccae47c41b08593ea6e22d92f9dc8 en feat/finite-readonly-preflight-20261103. Solo fuente y fixtures sintéticos; no se montó ruta, Workflow ni dispatch de observación real.\n+\n+El host fijo readFinitePreEntryCustody abre un ámbito read-only en el registro privado compartido, crea su propio plazo de como máximo 15 s y llama la observación existente con el actor y recursos fijos. Conserva comprobaciones de reloj y holders; los GET de token mantienen su límite de 5 s. La continuación owner rechaza el modo read-only y el registro excluye un segundo ámbito u owner mientras haya trabajo retenido. Al acabar o vencer el plazo el ámbito permanece ocupado hasta que se liquiden las promesas retenidas. No crea una admisión ni devuelve autoridad, credenciales o secretos; no cambia el techo de la fuente. El guard conserva las limitaciones de cancelación física descritas en el informe anterior.\n+\n+RED 57e3f7e641a8bdec41babc1414f9c2b7da042b41, Actions 41687696843: 15 PASS, 2 FAIL causales por stub cerrado. GREEN focal 03b100f1f817cd6921f65c28ac1d65bb334967c3, Actions 41687921174. La primera ejecución ampliada 41688158861 falló por salto literal en el YAML; corregido en a0351f. GREEN ampliado Actions 41688184338: nueve suites, 105 PASS, 0 FAIL, 0 SKIP y lint acotado aprobado.\n+\n+Las pruebas sintéticas verifican ausencia de Entry antes de la llamada, snapshot del actor sin cambios, ausencia de uploads/restores, rechazo de actor e identidades de tokens incorrectos, exclusión y rechazo de continuación owner, timeout y retención de GET pendiente. Los tests de registro usan objetos sintéticos para holders y leases; no demuestran liquidación remota ni constituyen inventario de escritores. No se realizó snapshot independiente de todas las tablas D1 en este nuevo fixture; la ruta nueva no emite SQL ni escrituras. La verificación real de custodia, calificación histórica de escritores, canal controlado y revisión de integración permanecen pendientes. operationalReadiness=false, no habilitación ni PC-off."
+    },
+    {
+      "path": "docs/superpowers/reports/2026-11-04-finite-readonly-integration.md",
+      "status": "added",
+      "additions": 32,

+      "patch": "@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@\n+# Finite readonly integration — source-only report\n+\n+PROJECT AFW; REPOSITORY tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web; ENVIRONMENT isolated source branch and credential-free GitHub Actions; ORIGIN delegated-canary.agentfriendlyweb.dev; RESOURCE_TYPE finite source and synthetic fixtures; ALLOWED_ACTION source integration and cloud qualification. Internal retention is not an admission. */\n+export async function readFinitePreEntryCustody(options){\n+ let controller,timer;\n+ try{\n+  fields(options,['ctx','env']);\n+  const {ctx,env}=options,storage=ctx.storage,db=env.RELEASE_DB;\n+  const start=Date.now(),mono=performance.now();let last=start,lastMono=mono;\n+  controller=new AbortController();\n+  const lease=Object.freeze({signal:controller.signal,assertCurrent:()=>{\n+   const now=Date.now(),m=performance.now();\n+   if(controller.signal.aborted||!Number.isSafeInteger(now)||now<last||now-start>=15000||!Number.isFinite(m)||m<lastMono||m-mono>=15000||ctx.storage!==storage||env.RELEASE_DB!==db)fail();\n+   last=now;lastMono=m;\n+  }});\n+  timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),15000);\n+  return await observeFiniteReadOnlyWork({ctx,env,originalLease:lease},()=>observeNativeCustody(options,lease));\n+ }catch{return unavailable();}finally{clearTimeout(timer);controller?.abort();}\n+}"

the exact original parent and holders must match. */\n export function continueFiniteOwnerStageWork({ctx,env,originalLease},operation){\n  const entry=outstanding.get(ctx);\n- if(!entry||entry.originalLease!==originalLease||entry.env!==env||entry.storage!==ctx.storage||entry.db!==env.RELEASE_DB||typeof operation!=='function')fail();\n+ if(!entry||entry.mode!=='owner'||entry.originalLease!==originalLease||entry.env!==env||entry.storage!==ctx.storage||entry.db!==env.RELEASE_DB||typeof operation!=='function')fail();\n  originalLease.assertCurrent();entry.admission.lease.assertCurrent();\n  if(originalLease.signal.aborted||entry.admission.lease.signal.aborted)fail();\n  return scopes.run(entry,()=>retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise(Promise.resolve().then(operation)));\n }\n+\n+/** Internal retention scope only: does not create or validate an admission. */\n+export function observeFiniteReadOnlyWork({ctx,env,originalLease},operation){\n+ if(!ctx||!env||typeof operation!=='function'||outstanding.has(ctx))fail();\n+ originalLease.assertCurrent();\n+ if(originalLease.signal.aborted)fail();\n+ const entry={mode:'read-only',ctx,env,storage:ctx.storage,db:env.RELEASE_DB,originalLease,promises:new Set(),release:null};\n+ entry.release=()=>released(entry);\n+ outstanding.set(ctx,entry);\n+ originalLease.signal.addEventListener('abort',entry.release,{once:true});\n+ return scopes.run(entry,()=>retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise(Promise.resolve().then(operation)));\n+}"
